4545/* Based on Casella-Tsukada paper. */
4546static void
4547set_dyes_to_autochrome2 (spectrum red, spectrum green, spectrum blue,
4548 luminosity_t orange_erythrosine, luminosity_t orange_rose, luminosity_t orange_tartrazine,
4549 luminosity_t green_patent, luminosity_t green_tartrazine,
4550 luminosity_t violet_crystal, luminosity_t violet_flexo,
4551 bool aged)
4552{
4553 spectrum tartrazine, rose, erythrosine, patent, flexo, crystal;
4554 spectrum o2_rose, o2_erythrosine, o2_patent, o2_flexo, o2_crystal;
4555 //compute_spectrum (tartrazine, 400, 700, sizeof tartrazine_data / sizeof (luminosity_t), tartrazine_data, false);
4556 compute_spectrum (tartrazine,
4557 sizeof (tartrazine_paper_data) / sizeof (spectra_entry),
4558 tartrazine_paper_data, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ -1, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4559 //compute_spectrum (rose, 400, 700, sizeof rose_data / sizeof (luminosity_t), rose_data, false);
4560 compute_spectrum (rose,
4561 sizeof (rose_paper_data) / sizeof (spectra_entry),
4562 rose_paper_data, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ -1, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4563 compute_spectrum (o2_rose,
4564 sizeof (o2_rose_paper_data) / sizeof (spectra_entry),
4565 o2_rose_paper_data, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ -1, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4566 //compute_spectrum (erythrosine, 400, 700, sizeof erythrosine_data / sizeof (luminosity_t), erythrosine_data, false);
4567 compute_spectrum (erythrosine,
4568 sizeof (erythrosine_paper_data) / sizeof (spectra_entry),
4569 erythrosine_paper_data, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ -1, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4570 compute_spectrum (o2_erythrosine,
4571 sizeof (o2_erythrosine_paper_data) / sizeof (spectra_entry),
4572 o2_erythrosine_paper_data, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ -1, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4573 //compute_spectrum (patent, 400, 700, sizeof patent_data / sizeof (luminosity_t), patent_data, false);
4574 compute_spectrum (patent,
4575 sizeof (patent_paper_data) / sizeof (spectra_entry),
4576 patent_paper_data, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ -1, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4577 compute_spectrum (o2_patent,
4578 sizeof (o2_patent_paper_data) / sizeof (spectra_entry),
4579 o2_patent_paper_data, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ -1, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4580 //compute_spectrum (flexo, 400, 700, sizeof flexo_data / sizeof (luminosity_t), flexo_data, false);
4581 compute_spectrum (flexo,
4582 sizeof (flexo_paper_data) / sizeof (spectra_entry),
4583 flexo_paper_data, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ -1, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4584 compute_spectrum (o2_flexo,
4585 sizeof (o2_flexo_paper_data) / sizeof (spectra_entry),
4586 o2_flexo_paper_data, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ -1, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4587 //compute_spectrum (crystal, 400, 700, sizeof crystal_data / sizeof (luminosity_t), crystal_data, false);
4588 compute_spectrum (crystal,
4589 sizeof (crystal_paper_data) / sizeof (spectra_entry),
4590 crystal_paper_data, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ -1, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4591 compute_spectrum (o2_crystal,
4592 sizeof (o2_crystal_paper_data) / sizeof (spectra_entry),
4593 o2_crystal_paper_data, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ -1, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4594 if (false)
4595 {
4596 FILE *f;
4597 f = fopen ("/tmp/tartrazine-trans.dat", "w");
4598 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, tartrazine);
4599 fclose (f);
4600 f = fopen ("/tmp/rose-trans.dat", "w");
4601 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, rose);
4602 fclose (f);
4603 f = fopen ("/tmp/o2_rose-trans.dat", "w");
4604 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, o2_rose);
4605 fclose (f);
4606 f = fopen ("/tmp/erythrosine-trans.dat", "w");
4607 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, erythrosine);
4608 fclose (f);
4609 f = fopen ("/tmp/o2_erythrosine-trans.dat", "w");
4610 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, o2_erythrosine);
4611 fclose (f);
4612 f = fopen ("/tmp/patent-trans.dat", "w");
4613 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, patent);
4614 fclose (f);
4615 f = fopen ("/tmp/o2_patent-trans.dat", "w");
4616 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, o2_patent);
4617 fclose (f);
4618 f = fopen ("/tmp/flexo-trans.dat", "w");
4619 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, flexo);
4620 fclose (f);
4621 f = fopen ("/tmp/o2_flexo-trans.dat", "w");
4622 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, o2_flexo);
4623 fclose (f);
4624 f = fopen ("/tmp/crystal-trans.dat", "w");
4625 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, crystal);
4626 fclose (f);
4627 f = fopen ("/tmp/o2_crystal-trans.dat", "w");
4628 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, o2_crystal);
4629 fclose (f);
4630 }
4631#define choose(n,m,w) my_pow ((aged ? m : n), w)
4632 const luminosity_t concentration = 3;
4633 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4634 {
4635 red[i] = my_pow ((choose (erythrosine[i], o2_erythrosine[i], orange_erythrosine)* choose (rose[i], o2_rose[i], orange_rose) * choose (tartrazine[i], tartrazine[i], orange_tartrazine)), concentration);
4636 green[i] = my_pow ((choose (patent[i], o2_patent[i], green_patent) * choose (tartrazine[i], tartrazine[i], green_tartrazine)), concentration);
4637 blue[i] = my_pow ((choose (crystal[i], o2_crystal[i], violet_crystal) * choose (flexo[i], o2_flexo[i], violet_flexo)), concentration);
4638 }
4639#undef choose
4640}
4641}

4644#define NORM 100000
4645#define SCALE 30
4646#define NORM2 9000000
4647void
4648set_synthetic_dufay_red (spectrum red, luminosity_t d1, luminosity_t d2)
4649{
4650#if 0
4651 static bool initialized;
4652 static spectrum auramine;
4653 static spectrum rhodamine_b;
4654 if (!initialized)
4655 {
4656 compute_spectrum (auramine, 380.0, 780.0, sizeof (auramine_absorption) / sizeof (luminosity_t), auramine_absorption, /* absorbance= */ true, /* norm= */ NORM, /* limit_range= */ true, /* clamp= */ false);
4657 compute_spectrum (rhodamine_b, 380.0, 780.0, sizeof (rhodamine_b_absorption) / sizeof (luminosity_t), rhodamine_b_absorption, /* absorbance= */ true, /* norm= */ NORM, /* limit_range= */ true, /* clamp= */ false);
4658 initialized = true;
4659 }
4660 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4661 red[i] = pow (rhodamine_b[i], d1/SCALE) * pow (auramine[i], d2/SCALE);
4662#endif
4663 assert (sizeof (auramine_absorption) / sizeof (luminosity_t) == SPECTRUM_SIZE);
4664 assert (sizeof (rhodamine_b_absorption) / sizeof (luminosity_t) == SPECTRUM_SIZE);
4665 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4666 red[i] = absorbance_to_transmitance (auramine_absorption[i] * d1/NORM2 + rhodamine_b_absorption[i] * d2/NORM2 );
4667}

4848 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4849 {
4850 red[i] *= response[i];
4851 green[i] *= response[i];
4852 blue[i] *= response[i];
4853 rsum += red[i];
4854 gsum += green[i];
4855 bsum += blue[i];
4856 }
4857 printf ("sums: %f %f %f\n", rsum, gsum, bsum);
4858 /* I do not know actual intensities of the LED lights, but they ought to look
4859 gray to calibrated scanner. */
4860 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4861 {
4862 red[i] /= rsum;
4863 green[i] /= gsum;
4864 blue[i] /= bsum;
4865 }
